### 1. user (2026-06-12T09:16:13.456Z)

In C:\Users\avidu\Projects\badminton-highlight-indexer: config.json's `stitching.begin_padding` / `stitching.end_padding` are silently ignored by the live highlight compiler. `backend/main.py` (around lines 508 and 633) constructs `VideoCompiler(output_dir, watermark=...)` WITHOUT passing the configured paddings, so the live stitcher always uses the constructor defaults in `backend/pipeline/stitcher/compiler.py:10` (begin=0.5, end=1.0). The config values are only read by `backend/pipeline/segmenters/gemini.py` (~lines 406-408) for a log-line duration estimate. This was audit finding (d) from the 2026-06-10 deep audit and was re-verified as still live on 2026-06-12. Fix: thread the typed config stitching paddings into both VideoCompiler construction sites, add a regression test asserting a config padding override changes the compiled clip boundaries. Note a cross-repo coupling: sports-data-collector `src/parsers/cvat/rally_cvat.py` detect_issues docstring (~lines 288-294) justifies not flagging vendor boundary slack by citing the compiler's 0.5/1.0 padding — if defaults/wiring change, update that rationale comment too. Follow repo conventions: branch off master, one PR, run `python run_all_tests.py`.

1	import os
2	import subprocess
3	import tempfile
4	import logging
5	from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Union
6	
7	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
8	
9	class VideoCompiler:
10	    def __init__(self, output_dir: str, end_padding: float = 1.0, begin_padding: float = 0.5,
11	                 watermark: Optional[Any] = None):
12	        self.output_dir = output_dir
13	        self.end_padding = end_padding
14	        self.begin_padding = begin_padding
15	        # Optional branding overlay. Accepts a WatermarkConfig model or a plain dict;
16	        # normalized to a dict (or None) so this module stays config-package-agnostic.
17	        self.watermark = self._normalize_watermark(watermark)
18	        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
19	
20	    @staticmethod
21	    def _normalize_watermark(watermark: Optional[Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
22	        if watermark is None:
23	            return None
24	        if hasattr(watermark, "model_dump"):
25	            return watermark.model_dump()
26	        if isinstance(watermark, dict):
27	            return dict(watermark)
28	        return None
29	
30	    @staticmethod
31	    def _ff_quote(path: str) -> str:
32	        """Make a filesystem path safe to embed inside single quotes in an ffmpeg
33	        filtergraph: forward slashes (Windows-friendly) + escaped single quotes."""
34	        return path.replace("\\", "/").replace("'", r"\'")
35	
36	    @staticmethod
37	    def _parse_time(val: Union[int, float, str]) -> float:
38	        """Normalizes a timestamp value to float seconds.
39	        Handles: float/int (pass-through), plain numeric strings ("12.4"),
40	        and colon-separated strings ("MM:SS", "HH:MM:SS", "H:MM:SS.ms").
41	        This mirrors the parse_time logic used in gemini.py to ensure
42	        the stitcher can always consume whatever format the indexer produced."""
43	        if isinstance(val, (int, float)):
44	            return float(val)
45	        if isinstance(val, str):
46	            val = val.strip()
47	            if ":" in val:
48	                # Handle MM:SS, HH:MM:SS, etc.
49	                parts = val.split(":")
50	                parts.reverse()
51	                total = 0.0
52	                for i, p in enumerate(parts):
53	                    try:
54	                        total += float(p) * (60 ** i)
55	                    except ValueError:
56	                        pass
57	                return total
58	            try:
59	                return float(val)
60	            except ValueError:
61	                logger.warning(f"Could not parse timestamp value '{val}' as a number. Defaulting to 0.0.")
62	                return 0.0
63	        logger.warning(f"Unexpected timestamp type {type(val).__name__} for value '{val}'. Defaulting to 0.0.")
64	        return 0.0
65	
66	    def _get_video_duration(self, video_path: str) -> float:
67	        """Probes the video file to get its total duration in seconds.
68	        Returns 0.0 if it cannot be determined."""
69	        try:
70	            result = subprocess.run(
71	                [
72	                    "ffprobe", "-v", "error",
73	                    "-show_entries", "format=duration",
74	                    "-of", "default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1",
75	                    video_path
76	                ],
77	                capture_output=True, text=True, check=True
78	            )
79	            return float(result.stdout.strip())
80	        except Exception as e:
81	            logger.warning(f"Could not determine video duration via ffprobe: {e}")
82	            return 0.0
83	
84	    def _watermark_filter(self, tmp_dir: str) -> Optional[str]:
85	        """Build the ffmpeg -vf filtergraph that burns the configured watermark (text
86	        and/or logo PNG) into a clip during the per-clip re-encode. Returns None when
87	        watermarking is disabled or has nothing to draw. The concat stage stays -c copy
88	        because the mark is already baked into every clip identically."""
89	        wm = self.watermark
90	        if not wm or not wm.get("enabled"):
91	            return None
92	
93	        text = (wm.get("text") or "").strip()
94	        logo_path = (wm.get("logo_path") or "").strip()
95	        if logo_path and not os.path.exists(logo_path):
96	            logger.warning(f"[watermark] logo not found: {logo_path} — skipping logo overlay.")
97	            logo_path = ""
98	        if not text and not logo_path:
99	            return None
100	
101	        margin = int(wm.get("margin", 24))
102	        opacity = float(wm.get("opacity", 0.85))
103	        position = str(wm.get("position", "bottom-right"))
104	        # drawtext uses lowercase w/h (main) + tw/th (text); overlay uses W/H (main) + w/h (logo).
105	        dt_xy = {
106	            "bottom-right": f"x=w-tw-{margin}:y=h-th-{margin}",
107	            "bottom-left": f"x={margin}:y=h-th-{margin}",
108	            "top-right": f"x=w-tw-{margin}:y={margin}",
109	            "top-left": f"x={margin}:y={margin}",
110	        }.get(position, f"x=w-tw-{margin}:y=h-th-{margin}")
111	        ov_xy = {
112	            "bottom-right": f"W-w-{margin}:H-h-{margin}",
113	            "bottom-left": f"{margin}:H-h-{margin}",
114	            "top-right": f"W-w-{margin}:{margin}",
115	            "top-left": f"{margin}:{margin}",
116	        }.get(position, f"W-w-{margin}:H-h-{margin}")
117	
118	        drawtext = ""
119	        if text:
120	            ratio = float(wm.get("font_size_ratio") or 0.035)
121	            divisor = max(1, round(1.0 / ratio))
122	            # textfile= sidesteps the notorious drawtext text= escaping for arbitrary strings.
123	            tf_path = os.path.join(tmp_dir, "watermark.txt")
124	            with open(tf_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
125	                fh.write(text)
126	            font_path = (wm.get("font_path") or "").strip()
127	            if font_path:
128	                font_opt = f"fontfile='{self._ff_quote(font_path)}'"
129	            else:
130	                font_opt = f"font='{wm.get('font', 'Sans')}'"
131	            box = ""
132	            if wm.get("box", True):
133	                box = f":box=1:boxcolor=black@{min(opacity, 0.5):.2f}:boxborderw=10"
134	            drawtext = (
135	                f"drawtext=textfile='{self._ff_quote(tf_path)}':{font_opt}"
136	                f":fontcolor=white@{opacity:.2f}:fontsize=h/{divisor}:{dt_xy}{box}"
137	            )
138	
139	        if logo_path and drawtext:
140	            return f"movie='{self._ff_quote(logo_path)}'[wm];[in][wm]overlay={ov_xy},{drawtext}"
141	        if logo_path:
142	            return f"movie='{self._ff_quote(logo_path)}'[wm];[in][wm]overlay={ov_xy}"
143	        return drawtext
144	
145	    def compile_highlights(self, raw_video_path: str, segments: List[Tuple[float, float]], output_filename: str) -> str:
146	        """
147	        Extracts selected segments from a raw video and stitches them together
148	        into a seamless highlights file, applying end_padding to prevent abrupt endings.
149	        """
150	        if not segments:
151	            raise ValueError("No highlight segments provided to compile.")
152	
153	        if not os.path.exists(raw_video_path):
154	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"[COMPILER ERROR] Source video file not found: {raw_video_path}")
155	
156	        # Defense-in-depth: never let the output name escape output_dir (the API also
157	        # validates this at the request boundary).
158	        output_path = os.path.join(self.output_dir, os.path.basename(output_filename))
159	
160	        # Probe video duration so we can detect out-of-range segments
161	        video_duration = self._get_video_duration(raw_video_path)
162	        if video_duration > 0:
163	            logger.info(f"Source video duration: {video_duration:.2f}s")
164	        else:
165	            logger.warning("Video duration unknown. Cannot validate segment boundaries against video length.")
166	
167	        # We will create temporary clips and stitch them using FFmpeg concat demuxer
168	        temp_clips = []
169	        skipped_segments = []
170	
171	        # Create a temp directory within output_dir
172	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=self.output_dir) as tmp_dir:
173	            logger.info(f"Trimming {len(segments)} segments (begin_padding={self.begin_padding}s, end_padding={self.end_padding}s)...")
174	            # Build the (optional) watermark filtergraph once; it is applied during every
175	            # per-clip re-encode so the mark is baked into each clip identically.
176	            watermark_filter = self._watermark_filter(tmp_dir)
177	            if watermark_filter:
178	                logger.info("[watermark] applying branding overlay to each clip.")
179	            for idx, (raw_start, raw_end) in enumerate(segments):
180	                # Normalize timestamps to float seconds — handles float, int,
181	                # plain numeric strings, and colon-separated formats like "MM:SS"
182	                start = self._parse_time(raw_start)
183	                end = self._parse_time(raw_end)
184	                segment_label = f"Segment #{idx+1}/{len(segments)} [{start:.2f}s - {end:.2f}s]"
185	
186	                # Validate the segment times
187	                if start < 0:
188	                    logger.warning(f"{segment_label}: start_time is negative ({start:.2f}s). Clamping to 0.")
189	                    start = 0.0
190	
191	                if start >= end:
192	                    logger.error(f"{segment_label}: start_time ({start:.2f}s) >= end_time ({end:.2f}s). Skipping invalid segment.")
193	                    skipped_segments.append((idx, start, end, "start >= end"))
194	                    continue
195	
196	                # Check if segment extends beyond video duration
197	                if video_duration > 0:
198	                    if start >= video_duration:
199	                        logger.error(f"{segment_label}: start_time ({start:.2f}s) is beyond the video duration ({video_duration:.2f}s). "
200	                                     f"This segment cannot be extracted — the video ran out. Skipping.")
201	                        skipped_segments.append((idx, start, end, "start beyond video end"))
202	                        continue
203	
204	                    if end > video_duration:
205	                        original_end = end
206	                        end = video_duration
207	                        logger.warning(f"{segment_label}: end_time ({original_end:.2f}s) exceeds video duration ({video_duration:.2f}s). "
208	                                       f"Clamping end_time to {video_duration:.2f}s. The segment may be truncated.")
209	
210	                clip_path = os.path.join(tmp_dir, f"clip_{idx}.mp4")
211	
212	                # Precise sub-second trim using fast re-encoding to preserve stream headers
213	                padded_start = max(0.0, start - self.begin_padding)
214	                padded_end = end + self.end_padding
215	
216	                # Clamp padded_end to video duration if known
217	                if video_duration > 0 and padded_end > video_duration:
218	                    padded_end = video_duration
219	                    logger.info(f"{segment_label}: Padded end ({end + self.end_padding:.2f}s) exceeds video duration. "
220	                                f"Clamping to {video_duration:.2f}s (end padding partially applied).")
221	
222	                trim_duration = padded_end - padded_start
223	                logger.info(f"Trimming {segment_label} -> [{padded_start:.3f}s - {padded_end:.3f}s] (duration: {trim_duration:.2f}s)")
224	
225	                base_cmd = [
226	                    "ffmpeg", "-y",
227	                    "-ss", str(round(padded_start, 3)),
228	                    "-to", str(round(padded_end, 3)),
229	                    "-i", raw_video_path,
230	                    "-c:v", "libx264",
231	                    "-preset", "superfast",
232	                    "-crf", "22",
233	                    "-c:a", "aac",
234	                    "-b:a", "128k",
235	                ]
236	                vf_args = ["-vf", watermark_filter] if watermark_filter else []
237	
238	                # Try with the watermark first; if that encode fails (e.g. a bad font/logo
239	                # path), retry once WITHOUT it so a branding misconfig can never nuke the
240	                # whole reel. Clips stay codec-identical either way, so concat -c copy holds.
241	                attempts = [vf_args, []] if vf_args else [[]]
242	                produced = False
243	                last_exc: Optional[subprocess.CalledProcessError] = None
244	                cmd = base_cmd + [clip_path]
245	                for attempt_vf in attempts:
246	                    cmd = base_cmd + attempt_vf + [clip_path]
247	                    try:
248	                        subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, check=True)
249	                    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
250	                        last_exc = e
251	                        if attempt_vf and vf_args:
252	                            logger.warning(f"{segment_label}: encode with watermark failed; retrying without it.")
253	                            continue
254	                        break
255	                    if os.path.exists(clip_path) and os.path.getsize(clip_path) > 0:
256	                        produced = True
257	                        if not attempt_vf and vf_args:
258	                            logger.warning(f"{segment_label}: encoded WITHOUT the watermark (the watermark filter failed).")
259	                        break
260	                    last_exc = None  # ffmpeg succeeded but produced nothing
261	                    break
262	
263	                if produced:
264	                    temp_clips.append(clip_path)
265	                elif last_exc is not None:
266	                    stderr_msg = last_exc.stderr.decode(errors='replace').strip()
267	                    logger.error(f"{segment_label}: FFmpeg trim failed.")
268	                    print(f"  Command: {' '.join(cmd)}")
269	                    print(f"  FFmpeg stderr: {stderr_msg[-500:]}")  # Last 500 chars to avoid flooding
270	                    skipped_segments.append((idx, start, end, f"ffmpeg error: {stderr_msg[-200:]}"))
271	                    continue
272	                else:
273	                    logger.error(f"{segment_label}: FFmpeg exited successfully but output clip is missing or empty. Skipping.")
274	                    skipped_segments.append((idx, start, end, "empty output clip"))
275	                    continue
276	
277	            # Report summary of skipped segments
278	            if skipped_segments:
279	                logger.info(f"\n[SUMMARY] {len(skipped_segments)} out of {len(segments)} segments were SKIPPED:")
280	                for seg_idx, seg_start, seg_end, reason in skipped_segments:
281	                    logger.info(f"  - Segment #{seg_idx+1} [{seg_start:.2f}s - {seg_end:.2f}s]: {reason}")
282	                logger.info("")
283	
284	            if not temp_clips:
285	                raise RuntimeError(
286	                    f"[COMPILER ERROR] Failed to generate ANY valid highlight clips. "
287	                    f"All {len(segments)} segments were skipped. "
288	                    f"Check the errors above for details. "
289	                    f"Common causes: video file is corrupted, segments are out of range, or ffmpeg is not installed."
290	                )
291	
292	            logger.info(f"Successfully trimmed {len(temp_clips)}/{len(segments)} clips. Preparing to stitch...")
293	
294	            # Create a list file for FFmpeg concat demuxer
295	            list_file_path = os.path.join(tmp_dir, "clips.txt")
296	            with open(list_file_path, "w") as f:
297	                for clip in temp_clips:
298	                    # Escape single quotes in path
299	                    safe_path = clip.replace("'", "'\\''")
300	                    f.write(f"file '{safe_path}'\n")
301	
302	            logger.info("Stitching clips together...")
303	            # Stitch them together without re-encoding (since they were encoded with identical settings)
304	            concat_cmd = [
305	                "ffmpeg", "-y",
306	                "-f", "concat",
307	                "-safe", "0",
308	                "-i", list_file_path,
309	                "-c", "copy",
310	                output_path
311	            ]
312	
313	            try:
314	                subprocess.run(concat_cmd, capture_output=True, check=True)
315	
316	                # Final verification
317	                if not os.path.exists(output_path) or os.path.getsize(output_path) == 0:
318	                    raise RuntimeError(
319	                        f"[COMPILER ERROR] FFmpeg concatenation appeared to succeed, "
320	                        f"but output file is missing or empty: {output_path}"
321	                    )
322	
323	                output_size_mb = os.path.getsize(output_path) / (1024 * 1024)
324	                logger.info(f"Dynamic highlight reel saved: {output_path} ({output_size_mb:.1f} MB)")
325	                return output_path
326	            except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
327	                stderr_msg = e.stderr.decode(errors='replace').strip()
328	                raise RuntimeError(
329	                    f"[COMPILER ERROR] FFmpeg concatenation failed.\n"
330	                    f"  Command: {' '.join(concat_cmd)}\n"
331	                    f"  FFmpeg stderr: {stderr_msg[-500:]}"
332	                ) from e

docs\CODE_MAP.md-156-- `@backend\utils\geometry.py` — `::get_velocity_normalised` (fraction of frame-width/s; ⚠ raw-px fallback if width<=0; used by yolo_hybrid), `::calculate_iou` (⚠ +1 px convention inflates IoU), `::get_center/get_distance/get_velocity`.

README.md:28:*   **High-Speed FFmpeg Stitching** with configurable `begin_padding`, `end_padding`, and `min_shot_count` filtering, plus an optional **branding watermark** burned into every reel (**on by default** — `"Generated by Khelsutra.guru"`, fully configurable, with an off switch).
README.md:104:`run_process_and_stitch.py` is an interactive convenience runner: it MD5-hashes a hardcoded video path, reuses cached indexed segments if available (offering to re-index from scratch), and then prompts for stitching parameters (`begin_padding`, `end_padding`, `min_shot_count`) before compiling the final highlight reel. Useful when iterating on stitching settings against a fixed large recording without re-running expensive segmentation.

471	@app.post("/api/query")
472	def query_play_segments(req: QueryRequest):
473	    filters = {
474	        "server": req.server,
475	        "receiver": req.receiver,
476	        "duration_min": req.duration_min,
477	        "event_type": req.event_type
478	    }
479	    segments = db_instance.query_play_segments(req.video_id, filters)
480	    return {"play_segments": segments}
481	
482	@app.post("/api/compile")
483	def compile_highlights(req: CompileRequest):
484	    video = db_instance.get_video(req.video_id)
485	    if not video:
486	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Indexed video record not found.")
487	
488	    # Retrieve matching play segments from db
489	    all_segments = db_instance.query_play_segments(req.video_id, {})
490	    matched_segments = [s for s in all_segments if s["id"] in req.segment_ids]
491	
492	    if not matched_segments:
493	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No matching play segments found to stitch.")
494	
495	    # Reject path traversal / absolute output names (os.path.join would otherwise let an
496	    # absolute or '..'-laden output_filename write anywhere on disk).
497	    try:
498	        out_name = safe_output_filename(req.output_filename)
499	    except ValueError as e:
500	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e)) from e
501	
502	    # Extract start/end time pairs
503	    time_pairs = [(s["start_time"], s["end_time"]) for s in matched_segments]
504	
505	    # Run compiler (with the optional configured branding watermark, if enabled)
506	    output_dir = getattr(getattr(global_config, "output", None), "output_dir", "output") or "output"
507	    watermark = getattr(getattr(global_config, "stitching", None), "watermark", None)
508	    compiler = VideoCompiler(output_dir, watermark=watermark)
509	    try:
510	        out_path = compiler.compile_highlights(video["filepath"], time_pairs, out_name)
511	        return {"status": "success", "filepath": out_path}
512	    except Exception as e:
513	        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Highlight stitching failed: {str(e)}") from e

516	def run_cli_diagnose_clip(video_path: str, timestamp: float):

283	def detect_issues(records: List[RallyRecord],
284	                  stop_frame: Optional[int] = None,
285	                  allowed_reasons: Optional[set] = None,
286	                  allowed_outcomes: Optional[set] = None,
287	                  max_gap_s: float = 15.0) -> List[Issue]:
288	    """Find the defects that block or degrade a clean ground-truth import.
289	
290	    Note: we deliberately do NOT flag the sampling density (step/fps). A boundary
291	    slack of ~0.5s is acceptable for this pipeline because the downstream
292	    highlight compiler pads every clip on output (badminton-highlight-indexer,
293	    backend/stitcher/compiler.py: begin_padding=0.5s, end_padding=1.0s), which
294	    absorbs the sampling uncertainty when highlights are trimmed.
295	    """
296	    allowed = allowed_reasons if allowed_reasons is not None else DEFAULT_ALLOWED_REASONS
297	    allowed_out = allowed_outcomes if allowed_outcomes is not None else DEFAULT_ALLOWED_OUTCOMES
298	    issues: List[Issue] = []
299	
300	    ordered = sorted(records, key=lambda r: r.rally_number)
301	    for i, r in enumerate(ordered):
302	        if r.end_time <= r.start_time:
303	            issues.append(Issue(
304	                "blocker", "zero_duration",
305	                f"Rally {r.rally_number}: end <= start "
306	                f"(frames {r.frame_min}-{r.frame_max}); single-sample or stray box.",
307	                r.rally_number))
308	
309	        if r.ending_reason is not None and r.ending_reason not in allowed:

62	
63	# Global variables initialized at startup

66	
67	# Async job executor (DEFERRED_HARDENING_PLAN #16). max_workers=1 on purpose: a single
68	# self-hosted box serializes GPU work, and the Gemini provider is rate-fragile under
69	# parallel uploads (2026-06-10 ops learning: serial uploads + backoff). The segmenters
70	# already parallelize their AI handoff internally via their own pools.
71	_job_executor: Optional[ThreadPoolExecutor] = None
72	
73	def _get_executor() -> ThreadPoolExecutor:
74	    """Lazy module-global executor; tests monkeypatch this for inline execution."""
75	    global _job_executor
76	    if _job_executor is None:
77	        _job_executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1, thread_name_prefix="jobworker")
78	    return _job_executor
79	
80	# Legacy thin wrapper for any remaining direct calls during transition.

21	def test_compile_basenames_output_and_stitches(tmp_path):
22	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "out"))
23	    raw = tmp_path / "raw.mp4"
24	    raw.write_bytes(b"x")
25	
26	    calls = []
27	
28	    def fake_run(cmd, *a, **k):
29	        calls.append(cmd)
30	        # Create whatever output file ffmpeg/ffprobe was told to write so existence checks pass.
31	        if cmd[0] == "ffprobe":
32	            return MagicMock(stdout="100.0\n", returncode=0)
33	        out = cmd[-1]
34	        if isinstance(out, str) and out.endswith((".mp4",)):
35	            open(out, "wb").write(b"clip")
36	        return MagicMock(returncode=0, stdout=b"", stderr=b"")
37	
38	    with patch.object(subprocess, "run", side_effect=fake_run):
39	        # Traversal in the name must be stripped (defense-in-depth basename).
40	        out_path = comp.compile_highlights(str(raw), [(0.0, 5.0), (10.0, 15.0)], "../reel.mp4")
41	
42	    assert os.path.basename(out_path) == "reel.mp4"
43	    assert os.path.dirname(out_path).endswith("out")
44	    # Per-clip trims + one concat = at least 3 ffmpeg invocations (plus an ffprobe).
45	    assert sum(1 for c in calls if c[0] == "ffmpeg") >= 3
46

96	- `@backend/config/models.py` — Pydantic v2 models; **single source of truth for defaults**.
97	  - `::AppConfig` — root. Scalars `sport='badminton'`, `ai_provider='gemini'`, `ai_model='gemini-2.5-flash'`; sections `validation`,`indexing`,`output`,`stitching` (`default_factory`). `model_config = {extra:'allow', validate_assignment:True}`. ⚠ `extra='allow'` -> unknown/typo'd config.json keys silently kept (legacy tolerance).
98	  - ⎇ `::AppConfig.get` — legacy dict-compat shim: returns nested **sections as plain dicts** (`model_dump(mode='json')`, NOT the model) so chained `config.get("indexing",{}).get(...)` keeps working; falls back to searching a leaf key in any section (sections discovered dynamically from `model_fields`). ⚠ "bit every validator on real runs" per docstring — returning the model breaks `.get`. `::AppConfig.__getitem__` raises `KeyError` for unknown top-level keys else delegates to `.get`.
99	  - `::IndexingConfig` — segmenter/detector knobs (`motion_threshold=0.08`, `min_segment_duration=3.0`, `dead_time_merge_gap=2.0`, `chunk_duration=90.0`, `chunk_overlap=10.0`, `yolo_velocity_threshold=0.15`, `yolo_frame_skip=3`, `ai_handoff_padding=2.0`, `ai_max_workers=5`, `log/store_yolo_candidates=True`, nested `tracknet`). ⚠ `default_segmenter='yolo_hybrid'` HERE but config.json overrides to `'gemini'`. ⎇ `::IndexingConfig.segmenter_must_be_registered` `@field_validator` is a **no-op pass-through** — a typo'd segmenter validates fine, only fails later at `segmenter_registry.get(...)`.
100	  - `::ValidationConfig` (`min_resolution_width=1280`,`min_resolution_height=720`,`min_fps=29.9`,`min_blur_threshold=20.0`,`max_scene_cuts_per_minute=0.5`, nested `relevance`); `::ValidationRelevanceConfig` (court HSV gates, `court_pixels_min_ratio=0.05`). `::TrackNetConfig` (WSL invocation contract: `wsl_distro='Ubuntu'`, `repo_dir`, `conda_env='TrackNetV4'`, `weights_path=''`, `queue_length=5`, `velocity_threshold=0.02`). `::OutputConfig` (`default_highlights_name`, `db_name='sports_indexer.db'`, `output_dir='output'` — CLI `--output-dir` overrides it on the typed model in `main()`). `::StitchingConfig` (`begin_padding=0.5`,`end_padding=0.5`,`use_cache=False`,`min_shot_count=1`). `::Sport` Literal of 5 sports. `::Config = AppConfig` alias.
101	- `@backend/config/loader.py` — load/save with defaults-merge.
102	  - `::DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH = Path("config.json")` (⚠ CWD-relative, NOT repo-root). `::_get_builtin_defaults` = `AppConfig().model_dump(...)`.
103	  - `::load_config` — precedence built-in-defaults -> file overrides. ⚠ **shallow top-level merge** (`{**defaults, **file_data}`): a section in config.json REPLACES the whole defaults dict for that section (Pydantic then re-applies field defaults for missing leaves). Missing/unreadable/parse-error file -> fully-defaulted AppConfig + `[CONFIG WARNING]` (non-fatal).

Done. The fix is shipped as two PRs, both CI-green and awaiting your review — I did not merge anything.

**[badminton-highlight-indexer #102](https://github.com/avidullu/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/102)** — the actual fix:
- Both `VideoCompiler` construction sites in [backend/main.py](backend/main.py) (`/api/compile` around line 508 and the CLI trim path around line 633) now pass `begin_padding`/`end_padding` from the typed `StitchingConfig`, alongside the watermark they already passed. The API site keeps the existing defensive idiom by falling back to `StitchingConfig()` when `global_config` is unset; `StitchingConfig` is now re-exported from `backend.config`.
- New regression test in [tests/test_api_endpoints.py](tests/test_api_endpoints.py) drives the **real** compiler through `/api/compile` with mocked ffmpeg/ffprobe and asserts that a config override of 2.0/3.0 actually moves the trim boundaries to `-ss 8.0 / -to 17.0` for a 10–14s segment — the previously unwired defaults would have produced 9.5/15.0, so this test fails on the old code.
- [docs/CODE_MAP.md](docs/CODE_MAP.md) truth-synced: the stitcher entry now distinguishes the constructor defaults (0.5/1.0) from the config-driven live call sites (0.5/0.5), and the padding recipe row points at the `main.py` wiring.
- Verified: `python run_all_tests.py` → **401 passed**; ruff and mypy clean on touched files; all 4 CI lanes green.

One behavior change worth knowing before merge: live API reels currently get 1.0s of end padding; with the shipped `config.json` they'll get the configured **0.5s** after this lands.

**[sports-data-collector #20](https://github.com/avidullu/sports-data-collector/pull/20)** — the cross-repo coupling you flagged: the `detect_issues` docstring in `rally_cvat.py` now cites the config-driven 0.5/0.5 paddings instead of the hardcoded 0.5/1.0 (and the corrected module path). Docstring-only; collector suite 137 passed, CI green. Your uncommitted `.gitignore` edit there was left untouched.

Both checkouts are back on `master`, and I recorded a checkpoint in the memory current-state file (noting the padding-rationale interaction with the in-flight Roora boundary-slack thread, since that rationale now rests on 0.5s rather than 1.0s of end padding). One observation: an untracked `arial.ttf` appeared at the indexer repo root — not from this work; I left it alone, but it may be a stray from a watermark-font experiment.

### 57. assistant (2026-06-12T09:46:59.126Z)

Done. The rule now lives at the top of your global `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`, so it loads in every session across all projects:

- **Always `git pull` before starting to read a repo** — at session start for the working directory, and again for any sibling repo (like `sports-data-collector`) the moment work touches it.
- **Safe form only:** `git pull --ff-only` — never auto-merge, rebase, or discard local state to make a pull succeed.
- **If it can't fast-forward** (diverged branch, dirty tree): report the state and proceed read-only on local, flagged as possibly stale — uncommitted changes are treated as a sibling session's or your WIP and never clobbered.

I also captured your *why* in the rule itself — parallel "clean slates" mean master moves between sessions — so future sessions understand the intent, not just the mechanic.

And it paid off immediately: running the pull on both repos just now, both masters were already current, but the fetch surfaced a new remote branch `claude/watermark-bundled-font` from another session — which neatly explains the stray `arial.ttf` I'd flagged at the indexer root earlier (it's that session's watermark-font experiment, left alone).

One option if you ever want this enforced mechanically rather than by instruction: a `SessionStart` hook in `settings.json` could run the `--ff-only` pull automatically before I even wake up. I'd lean against it — a hook pulls blindly without first checking for a dirty tree or a sibling session mid-work on a feature branch, whereas the instruction lets me look before pulling — but say the word and I'll set it up.

21	def main():
22	    video_path = r"C:\Users\avidu\Videos\Testing\KushagraYashAviFirstVideo.mp4"
23	    output_dir = r"C:\Users\avidu\Videos\Testing"
24	    output_filename = "Final_2_OutputOfKushagraYashAviFirstVideo.mp4"
25	
26	    print("Loading config...")
27	    cfg = load_config()  # typed AppConfig — single source of defaults
28	
29	    print("Verifying files exist...")
30	    if not os.path.exists(video_path):
31	        print(f"Error: Video file not found at {video_path}")
32	        return
33	
34	    os.makedirs(cfg.output.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
35	    db_path = os.path.join(cfg.output.output_dir, cfg.output.db_name)
36	    db = Database(db_path)
37	
38	    # Calculate MD5 of the video to uniquely identify it
39	    import time
40	    print("Calculating video MD5 (this might take a few seconds on large files)...", flush=True)
41	    t0 = time.time()
42	    video_id = compute_video_id(video_path)
43	    print(f"Calculated MD5: {video_id} (took {time.time()-t0:.1f}s)", flush=True)
44	
45	    segments = []
46	    cached_video = db.get_video(video_id)
47	    cache_hit = False
48	
49	    if cached_video and cached_video.get("status") == "processed":
50	        segments = db.query_play_segments(video_id, {})
51	        if len(segments) > 0:
52	            print(f"\n[CACHE] Found cached indexed video in database (MD5: {video_id}) with {len(segments)} parsed play segments.", flush=True)
53	            choice = input("Would you like to (1) Run stitching on the cached segments, or (2) Reprocess the video with Gemini from scratch? [1/2]: ").strip()
54	            if choice == '1':
55	                print("[CACHE] Using cached play segments. Skipping segmenter API call.", flush=True)
56	                cache_hit = True
57	            else:
58	                print("[CACHE] Ignoring cache...", flush=True)
59	        else:
60	            print("[CACHE] Video exists in DB but contains 0 play segments. Treating as cache miss.", flush=True)
61	
62	    if not cache_hit:
63	        print("[CACHE] Initializing clean indexing...", flush=True)
64	        db.clear_video_data(video_id)
65	
66	        print("Bypassing OpenCV validations for performance on massive files...", flush=True)
67	        db.add_video(video_id, video_path, "processed", [])
68	
69	        print("Running segmenter & indexing...", flush=True)
70	        seg_name = cfg.indexing.default_segmenter
71	        segmenter_cls = segmenter_registry.get(seg_name)
72	        if not segmenter_cls:
73	            print(f"[FAIL] Segmenter '{seg_name}' not found.")
74	            return
75	        print(f"Using segmenter: {seg_name}", flush=True)
76	        segmenter = segmenter_cls(db, cfg)
77	        segments = segmenter.process_video(video_id, video_path)
78	        print(f"Successfully indexed {len(segments)} play segments.", flush=True)
79	
80	    if not segments:
81	        print("No play segments detected. Cannot compile highlights.")
82	        return
83	
84	
85	    end_padding = cfg.stitching.end_padding
86	    begin_padding = cfg.stitching.begin_padding
87	    min_shot_count = cfg.stitching.min_shot_count
88	
89	    if cache_hit:
90	        print("\n--- Stitching Customizations ---")
91	        try:
92	            bpad_input = input(f"Enter begin_padding in seconds (default {begin_padding}): ").strip()
93	            if bpad_input:
94	                begin_padding = float(bpad_input)
95	
96	            pad_input = input(f"Enter end_padding in seconds (default {end_padding}): ").strip()
97	            if pad_input:
98	                end_padding = float(pad_input)
99	
100	            shot_input = input(f"Enter min_shot_count to filter by (default {min_shot_count}): ").strip()
101	            if shot_input:
102	                min_shot_count = int(shot_input)
103	        except ValueError:
104	            print("[WARNING] Invalid input detected, falling back to defaults from config.json.")
105	
106	    print(f"\nCompiling highlights (with begin_padding={begin_padding}s, end_padding={end_padding}s, min_shot_count={min_shot_count})...", flush=True)
107	
108	    # Filter segments where shot_count >= min_shot_count
109	    time_pairs = []
110	    skipped_count = 0
111	    for s in segments:
112	        meta = s.get("metadata", {})
113	        if isinstance(meta, str):
114	            try:
115	                meta = json.loads(meta)
116	            except Exception:
117	                meta = {}
118	        shot_count = meta.get("shot_count", 0)
119	        if shot_count >= min_shot_count:
120	            time_pairs.append((s["start_time"], s["end_time"]))
121	        else:
122	            skipped_count += 1
123	
124	    if skipped_count > 0:
125	        print(f"[FILTER] Excluded {skipped_count} play segments with fewer than {min_shot_count} shots.", flush=True)
126	    print(f"[FILTER] Retaining {len(time_pairs)} play segments for stitching.", flush=True)
127	
128	    if not time_pairs:
129	        print("No play segments passed the filtering criteria. Cannot compile highlights.")
130	        return
131	
132	    compiler = VideoCompiler(output_dir, end_padding=end_padding, begin_padding=begin_padding,
133	                             watermark=cfg.stitching.watermark)
134	    try:
135	        out_path = compiler.compile_highlights(video_path, time_pairs, output_filename)
136	        print(f"Stitched highlight video saved to: {out_path}", flush=True)
137	    except Exception as e:
138	        print(f"Error during stitching: {e}", flush=True)
139	
140	if __name__ == "__main__":
141	    main()
142

### 76. assistant (2026-06-12T16:47:25.758Z)

"""One-off non-interactive E2E runner for GX010125.MP4 (modeled on run_process_and_stitch.py).

Every knob — segmenter, min_shot_count, paddings, watermark — comes from config.json.
Nothing is overridden here, so the run demonstrates the config is actually picked up.
"""
import json
import logging
import os
import time

from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()

logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s [%(name)s] %(levelname)s: %(message)s",
    datefmt="%H:%M:%S",
)

from backend.infrastructure.database import Database
from backend.config import load_config
from backend.utils.hashing import compute_video_id
from backend.pipeline.segmenters import segmenter_registry
from backend.pipeline.stitcher.compiler import VideoCompiler

VIDEO_PATH = r"F:\GoPro Hero Black 12\KhelSutra\Badminton 12 June 2026\GX010125.MP4"
OUTPUT_FILENAME = "GX010125_highlights.mp4"


def main():
    cfg = load_config()
    print(f"[CONFIG] segmenter={cfg.indexing.default_segmenter} | "
          f"min_shot_count={cfg.stitching.min_shot_count} | "
          f"begin_padding={cfg.stitching.begin_padding}s end_padding={cfg.stitching.end_padding}s | "
          f"watermark={'on' if cfg.stitching.watermark.enabled else 'off'}", flush=True)

    if not os.path.exists(VIDEO_PATH):
        raise SystemExit(f"video not found: {VIDEO_PATH}")

    os.makedirs(cfg.output.output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    db = Database(os.path.join(cfg.output.output_dir, cfg.output.db_name))

    t0 = time.time()
    print("[HASH] computing MD5 of the source video (11.5 GB — takes a while)...", flush=True)
    video_id = compute_video_id(VIDEO_PATH)
    print(f"[HASH] video_id={video_id} ({time.time() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)

    segments = []
    cached = db.get_video(video_id)
    if cached and cached.get("status") == "processed":
        segments = db.query_play_segments(video_id, {})
        if segments:
            print(f"[CACHE] reusing {len(segments)} cached segments for {video_id}", flush=True)

    if not segments:
        print("[INDEX] clean indexing (validators bypassed for the massive file, "
              "as in run_process_and_stitch.py)...", flush=True)
        db.clear_video_data(video_id)
        db.add_video(video_id, VIDEO_PATH, "processed", [])
        seg_name = cfg.indexing.default_segmenter
        segmenter_cls = segmenter_registry.get(seg_name)
        if not segmenter_cls:
            raise SystemExit(f"segmenter '{seg_name}' not registered")
        segmenter = segmenter_cls(db, cfg)
        segments = segmenter.process_video(video_id, VIDEO_PATH)
        print(f"[INDEX] {len(segments)} play segments indexed "
              f"(total {time.time() - t0:.0f}s).", flush=True)

    if not segments:
        raise SystemExit("no play segments detected — nothing to stitch")

    min_shots = cfg.stitching.min_shot_count
    time_pairs, skipped = [], 0
    for s in segments:
        meta = s.get("metadata") or {}
        if isinstance(meta, str):
            try:
                meta = json.loads(meta)
            except Exception:
                meta = {}
        sc = meta.get("shot_count", 0)
        line = (f"  segment id={s.get('id')} "
                f"[{float(s['start_time']):8.2f}s - {float(s['end_time']):8.2f}s] "
                f"shot_count={sc} confidence={meta.get('shot_count_confidence', '?')}")
        if sc >= min_shots:
            time_pairs.append((s["start_time"], s["end_time"]))
            print(line + "  -> KEEP", flush=True)
        else:
            skipped += 1
            print(line + "  -> drop", flush=True)

    print(f"[FILTER] config min_shot_count={min_shots}: "
          f"kept {len(time_pairs)}, dropped {skipped}", flush=True)
    if not time_pairs:
        raise SystemExit(f"no segments passed the min_shot_count={min_shots} filter")

    compiler = VideoCompiler(cfg.output.output_dir,
                             begin_padding=cfg.stitching.begin_padding,
                             end_padding=cfg.stitching.end_padding,
                             watermark=cfg.stitching.watermark)
    out = compiler.compile_highlights(VIDEO_PATH, time_pairs, OUTPUT_FILENAME)
    print(f"[DONE] highlight reel: {os.path.abspath(out)} (total {time.time() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

### 88. assistant (2026-06-12T16:49:01.064Z)

The end-to-end run is **launched and being monitored**. Here's the setup and what to expect:

**What's running** (background job `bwqevfnlg`, log at [output/gx010125_run.log](output/gx010125_run.log)):
1. **MD5 hash** of the 11.5 GB source (this is the `video_id`) — a few minutes.
2. **WASB shuttle-trajectory detection on the GPU** (RTX 2070 Super via WSL2 Ubuntu, pretrained badminton weights — verified present) over the full 12.8-minute 4K60 video → candidate rally windows.
3. **Gemini confirmation** of each candidate window (serial uploads with backoff, re-encoded 1280p chunks) → confirmed rallies **with per-rally `shot_count`** (Gemini's shuttle-exchange count; duration-based estimate only if the model omits it).
4. **Filter + stitch**: only rallies with `shot_count >= 8` (your ">7 shots") survive, then the compiler cuts and concatenates them with the configured paddings and watermark into `output/GX010125_highlights.mp4`.

**Your config question — yes, it's possible, with one nuance.** `stitching.min_shot_count` is honored by the offline runner flow (`run_process_and_stitch.py` lineage), which is what I'm using — I set it to **8** in [config.json](config.json) and the runner reads it from config with no override, logging each segment's shot count and KEEP/drop decision so you can audit the filter. The nuance: the **API path** (`/api/compile`) does *not* apply `min_shot_count` — it stitches exactly the `segment_ids` the caller picks (the UI is expected to filter). If you want the config filter enforced on the API path too, that's a small PR — say the word.

**Config changes made** (local, uncommitted): `indexing.default_segmenter: gemini → wasb_hybrid` (the GPU path you asked for) and `stitching.min_shot_count: 1 → 8`. Also synced to head first — your merge of the padding fix (#102) means this run uses the now config-driven 0.5s paddings.

Expect roughly 30–60+ minutes total (GPU inference on ~46k frames is the long pole). The monitor will report each phase as it lands; I'll summarize the kept rallies and the reel path when it finishes.

1	"""WASB shuttle-trajectory runner (WSL2 subprocess adapter).
2	
3	Windows-side counterpart to backend/detectors/wasb_infer.py. It:
4	  1. stages the video into the WSL filesystem (fast local reads, not /mnt),
5	  2. copies wasb_infer.py into the WASB repo's src/ dir,
6	  3. runs it in the WSL `wasb` conda env -> a Frame,Visibility,X,Y trajectory CSV,
7	  4. reuses the (model-agnostic) trajectory->action-window logic from tracknet_runner.
8	
9	So the only WASB-specific bits live here; the windowing + CSV parsing are shared
10	with the TrackNet path. Verified inference core: wasb_infer.py (see its docstring).
11	"""
12	import os
13	import shlex
14	import logging
15	import subprocess
16	from dataclasses import dataclass
17	from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, Tuple
18	
19	# Reuse the model-agnostic helpers — trajectory shape + windowing are identical.
20	# parse_trajectory_csv / trajectory_to_action_windows are @staticmethods on TrackNetRunner.
21	from backend.pipeline.detectors.tracknet_runner import to_wsl_mnt_path, TrackNetRunner
22	from backend.pipeline.detectors.base import DetectorRunner, WslCommandMixin, wsl_tilde_quote
23	
24	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
25	
26	# Windows path to the inference wrapper we stage into WSL.
27	_INFER_WIN = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "wasb_infer.py")
28	
29	
30	@dataclass
31	class WasbConfig:
32	    """Resolved settings for a WASB WSL run (built from config.json -> indexing.wasb)."""
33	    distro: str = "Ubuntu"
34	    repo_dir: str = "~/models/WASB-SBDT"
35	    conda_sh: str = "~/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh"
36	    conda_env: str = "wasb"
37	    weights_path: str = "~/models/WASB-SBDT/pretrained_weights/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar"
38	    sport: str = "badminton"
39	    wsl_stage_dir: str = "~/clips"
40	    timeout_sec: int = 1800  # 30 min; a hung GPU/conda call is killed (0 = no timeout)
41	
42	    @classmethod
43	    def from_indexing_cfg(cls, idx_cfg: Dict[str, Any]) -> "WasbConfig":
44	        w = (idx_cfg or {}).get("wasb", {}) or {}
45	        return cls(
46	            distro=w.get("wsl_distro", "Ubuntu"),
47	            repo_dir=w.get("repo_dir", "~/models/WASB-SBDT"),
48	            conda_sh=w.get("conda_sh", "~/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh"),
49	            conda_env=w.get("conda_env", "wasb"),
50	            weights_path=w.get("weights_path",
51	                               "~/models/WASB-SBDT/pretrained_weights/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar"),
52	            sport=w.get("sport", "badminton"),
53	            wsl_stage_dir=w.get("wsl_stage_dir", "~/clips"),
54	            timeout_sec=int(w.get("timeout_sec", 1800)),
55	        )
56	
57	
58	class WasbRunner(WslCommandMixin, DetectorRunner):
59	    def __init__(self, cfg: WasbConfig):
60	        self.cfg = cfg
61	
62	    def healthcheck(self) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
63	        """Verify the WSL `wasb` env imports torch and sees a GPU. Returns (ok, msg)."""
64	        cmd = (f"conda activate {self.cfg.conda_env} && "
65	               f"python -c \"import torch; print('torch', torch.__version__, "
66	               f"'cuda', torch.cuda.is_available())\"")
67	        try:
68	            res = self._wsl_bash(cmd)
69	        except FileNotFoundError:
70	            return False, "`wsl` not found — Windows + WSL2 required."
71	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
72	            return False, "WSL healthcheck timed out."
73	        out = (res.stdout or "").strip()
74	        if res.returncode != 0:
75	            return False, f"WSL `wasb` env check failed: {(res.stderr or out).strip()}"
76	        return True, out
77	
78	    def _stage_video(self, video_win_path: str, dest_name: str) -> Optional[str]:
79	        src_mnt = to_wsl_mnt_path(video_win_path)
80	        dest = f"{self.cfg.wsl_stage_dir}/{dest_name}"
81	        cmd = f"mkdir -p {self.cfg.wsl_stage_dir} && cp {shlex.quote(src_mnt)} {wsl_tilde_quote(dest)} && echo OK"
82	        try:
83	            res = self._wsl_bash(cmd)
84	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
85	            logger.error("Staging video into WSL timed out.")
86	            return None
87	        if res.returncode != 0 or "OK" not in (res.stdout or ""):
88	            logger.error("Failed to stage video into WSL: %s", (res.stderr or res.stdout).strip())
89	            return None
90	        return dest
91	
92	    def _stage_infer_script(self) -> bool:
93	        """Copy wasb_infer.py into the WASB repo src/ so it can import WASB modules."""
94	        src_mnt = to_wsl_mnt_path(_INFER_WIN)
95	        cmd = f"cp {shlex.quote(src_mnt)} {self.cfg.repo_dir}/src/wasb_infer.py && echo OK"
96	        res = self._wsl_bash(cmd)
97	        return res.returncode == 0 and "OK" in (res.stdout or "")
98	
99	    def run_predict(self, video_win_path: str, output_win_dir: str,
100	                    video_id: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[str]:
101	        """Run WASB on a video. Returns the Windows path to the trajectory CSV, or None.
102	
103	        All WSL/cache/output artifacts are namespaced by ``video_id`` (the file MD5) so
104	        two videos that share a filename (e.g. two ``match.mp4`` from different folders)
105	        can never reuse each other's staged frames, resumable cache, or CSV.
106	        """
107	        # Resolve relative dirs (the hybrids pass "output/wasb") BEFORE the /mnt
108	        # translation: to_wsl_mnt_path passes relative paths through unchanged, so
109	        # WSL resolved them against the inference cwd (~/models/WASB-SBDT/src) and
110	        # the CSV landed inside WSL while Windows polled the repo-relative path.
111	        output_win_dir = os.path.abspath(output_win_dir)
112	        os.makedirs(output_win_dir, exist_ok=True)
113	        # Normalize for cross-platform basename (tests use Windows paths on Linux).
114	        video_win_path = str(video_win_path).replace("\\", "/")
115	        base = os.path.basename(video_win_path)
116	        stem, ext = os.path.splitext(base)
117	        # Unique, content-derived key: same filename + different bytes -> different key.
118	        key = f"{stem}__{video_id[:12]}" if video_id else stem
119	        wsl_out_dir = to_wsl_mnt_path(output_win_dir)
120	        expected_csv_win = os.path.join(output_win_dir, f"{key}_wasb.csv")
121	
122	        if not self._stage_infer_script():
123	            logger.error("Could not stage wasb_infer.py into WSL.")
124	            return None
125	        staged_video = self._stage_video(video_win_path, f"{key}{ext}")
126	        if staged_video is None:
127	            return None
128	        frames_dir = f"{self.cfg.wsl_stage_dir}/{key}_frames"
129	
130	        cmd = (
131	            f"conda activate {self.cfg.conda_env} && cd {self.cfg.repo_dir}/src && "
132	            f"python wasb_infer.py "
133	            f"--video {wsl_tilde_quote(staged_video)} "
134	            f"--frames_out_dir {wsl_tilde_quote(frames_dir)} "
135	            f"--weights {wsl_tilde_quote(self.cfg.weights_path)} "
136	            f"--sport {shlex.quote(self.cfg.sport)} "
137	            f"--out {wsl_tilde_quote(wsl_out_dir + '/' + key + '_wasb.csv')}"
138	        )
139	        logger.info("Running WASB inference on %s ...", base)
140	        try:
141	            res = self._wsl_bash(cmd)
142	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
143	            logger.error("WASB inference timed out after %ss.", self.cfg.timeout_sec)
144	            return None
145	        if res.returncode != 0:
146	            logger.error("WASB inference failed:\n%s", (res.stderr or res.stdout)[-2000:])
147	            return None
148	        if not os.path.exists(expected_csv_win):
149	            logger.error("WASB finished but expected CSV not found at %s", expected_csv_win)
150	            return None
151	        logger.info("WASB trajectory CSV: %s", expected_csv_win)
152	        return expected_csv_win
153	
154	    # Trajectory parsing + windowing are inherited from tracknet_runner (model-agnostic).
155	    parse_trajectory_csv = staticmethod(TrackNetRunner.parse_trajectory_csv)
156	    trajectory_to_action_windows = staticmethod(TrackNetRunner.trajectory_to_action_windows)

### 172. user (2026-06-13T08:24:00.822Z)

1	"""WASB shuttle-trajectory inference on an arbitrary frame sequence (runs in WSL).
2	
3	WASB-SBDT only ships *dataset* evaluation; this is a thin wrapper that reuses its
4	own building blocks (ImageDataset transform + detector + online tracker) to run on
5	any folder of extracted frames and emit a trajectory CSV in the exact shape the
6	indexer's `tracknet_runner.parse_trajectory_csv` already consumes:
7	
8	    Frame,Visibility,X,Y
9	
10	It must run INSIDE the WSL `wasb` conda env, from the WASB-SBDT `src/` dir (it
11	imports WASB's `detectors`/`trackers`/`dataloaders`). The Windows-side adapter
12	(`wasb_runner.py`) stages this file + the frames into WSL and invokes it.
13	
14	Resumable across reboots
15	------------------------
16	The GPU detector pass (one forward per sliding window — ~21k for a 6-min clip) is
17	the slow, expensive part; the CPU tracker pass that turns detections into a
18	trajectory is cheap and *stateful* (it must see every frame in order, so it can't
19	resume mid-stream). We exploit that split:
20	
21	  * The detector pass writes its per-window raw outputs incrementally to
22	    ``<cache>/det_raw.jsonl`` (flushed+fsync'd every ``--flush-every`` windows) and
23	    records progress in ``<cache>/manifest.json`` (atomic write). A reboot loses at
24	    most ``--flush-every`` windows of GPU work instead of the whole pass.
25	  * On restart we replay the cached windows, skip the DataLoader ahead to the first
26	    un-cached window, and continue. Once every window is cached, the detector pass
27	    is skipped entirely and we just re-run the (cheap, deterministic) tracker over
28	    the full ordered cache -> trajectory CSV. So a lost trajectory CSV costs seconds,
29	    not the GPU hour.
30	
31	The cache is keyed by the output path (``<out>_wasbcache/`` by default), lives next
32	to the output (NOT in %TEMP%), and is invalidated automatically if the frames dir,
33	weights, sport, window size, or frame count change.
34	
35	Usage (in WSL, from ~/models/WASB-SBDT/src):
36	    python wasb_infer.py --frames_dir /path/frames --weights ../pretrained_weights/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar \
37	        --out /path/traj.csv [--sport badminton] [--limit N] [--cache-dir DIR] [--flush-every 1000] [--fresh]
38	"""
39	import os
40	import os.path as osp
41	import csv
42	import glob
43	import json
44	import argparse
45	import logging
46	from collections import defaultdict
47	
48	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
49	
50	CACHE_VERSION = 1
51	_DET_FILE = "det_raw.jsonl"
52	_MANIFEST_FILE = "manifest.json"
53	
54	
55	def _build_cfg(weights: str, sport: str):
56	    """Compose the WASB eval config via Hydra, overriding model/weights/device."""
57	    from hydra import compose, initialize
58	    with initialize(config_path="configs", version_base=None):
59	        cfg = compose(config_name="eval", overrides=[
60	            f"dataset={sport}",
61	            "model=wasb",
62	            f"detector.model_path={weights}",
63	            "runner.device=cuda",
64	            "runner.gpus=[0]",      # this box has a single GPU; default config assumes more
65	        ])
66	    return cfg
67	
68	
69	def _frame_id(path: str) -> int:
70	    """Best-effort integer frame id from a frame filename (e.g. frame_000180.png -> 180)."""
71	    stem = osp.splitext(osp.basename(path))[0]
72	    digits = "".join(ch for ch in stem if ch.isdigit())
73	    return int(digits) if digits else -1
74	
75	
76	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
77	# Resumable detector cache (pure-Python, no torch/numpy — unit-testable)
78	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
79	def _cache_paths(cache_dir: str):
80	    return osp.join(cache_dir, _DET_FILE), osp.join(cache_dir, _MANIFEST_FILE)
81	
82	
83	def default_cache_dir(out_csv: str) -> str:
84	    """Stable cache dir next to the trajectory output (survives reboots; not %TEMP%)."""
85	    d = osp.dirname(osp.abspath(out_csv))
86	    stem = osp.splitext(osp.basename(out_csv))[0]
87	    return osp.join(d, stem + "_wasbcache")
88	
89	
90	def _atomic_write_text(path: str, text: str) -> None:
91	    """Write then rename so a crash mid-write can't leave a half-written file."""
92	    tmp = path + ".tmp"
93	    with open(tmp, "w") as f:
94	        f.write(text)
95	        f.flush()
96	        os.fsync(f.fileno())
97	    os.replace(tmp, path)
98	
99	
100	def _det_plain(det) -> list:
101	    """A detector candidate dict {'xy': np.array([x,y]), 'score', 'scale'} -> JSON-able list."""
102	    xy = det["xy"]
103	    return [float(xy[0]), float(xy[1]), float(det["score"]), int(det["scale"])]
104	
105	
106	def _dets_to_tracker(plain_list):
107	    """Inverse of _det_plain: rebuild the dicts the tracker expects (xy MUST be a numpy array,
108	    the tracker does vector math / np.linalg.norm on it)."""
109	    import numpy as np
110	    return [{"xy": np.array([p[0], p[1]], dtype=float), "score": p[2], "scale": p[3]}
111	            for p in plain_list]
112	
113	
114	def write_manifest(cache_dir: str, manifest: dict) -> None:
115	    _, mpath = _cache_paths(cache_dir)
116	    _atomic_write_text(mpath, json.dumps(manifest, indent=2))
117	
118	
119	def read_manifest(cache_dir: str):
120	    _, mpath = _cache_paths(cache_dir)
121	    if not osp.exists(mpath):
122	        return None
123	    try:
124	        with open(mpath) as f:
125	            return json.load(f)
126	    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
127	        return None
128	
129	
130	def manifest_compatible(manifest: dict, *, frames_dir: str, weights: str, sport: str,
131	                        frames_in: int, frames_total: int) -> bool:
132	    """A cache is reusable only if the run that produced it matches this run's inputs."""
133	    if not manifest or manifest.get("version") != CACHE_VERSION:
134	        return False
135	    return (
136	        manifest.get("frames_dir") == osp.abspath(frames_dir)
137	        and manifest.get("weights") == weights
138	        and manifest.get("sport") == sport
139	        and manifest.get("frames_in") == frames_in
140	        and manifest.get("frames_total") == frames_total
141	    )
142	
143	
144	def reset_cache(cache_dir: str) -> None:
145	    """Drop any existing cache so the next run starts clean."""
146	    det_jsonl, mpath = _cache_paths(cache_dir)
147	    for p in (det_jsonl, mpath):
148	        if osp.exists(p):
149	            os.remove(p)
150	
151	
152	def load_and_clean_cache(cache_dir: str):
153	    """Read the longest *contiguous* (w == line index) valid prefix of det_raw.jsonl,
154	    rebuild the per-frame detection accumulation, and rewrite the file to exactly that
155	    prefix so a trailing half-written line from a crash can't corrupt future appends.
156	
157	    Returns (windows_done, det_by_fid) where det_by_fid maps frame_id -> list of
158	    [x, y, score, scale] candidates (accumulated across every window the frame is in,
159	    in window order — identical to a non-resumed run).
160	    """
161	    det_jsonl, _ = _cache_paths(cache_dir)
162	    det_by_fid = defaultdict(list)
163	    valid: list = []
164	    if osp.exists(det_jsonl):
165	        with open(det_jsonl, "r") as f:
166	            for line in f:
167	                s = line.strip()
168	                if not s:
169	                    continue
170	                try:
171	                    obj = json.loads(s)
172	                except json.JSONDecodeError:
173	                    break                       # truncated trailing line (crash mid-flush)
174	                if obj.get("w") != len(valid):  # non-contiguous -> stop at the gap
175	                    break
176	                valid.append(s)
177	                for fid, plain in obj["f"]:
178	                    det_by_fid[int(fid)].extend([list(p) for p in plain])
179	        # Guarantee a clean append boundary by rewriting only the good prefix.
180	        _atomic_write_text(det_jsonl, ("\n".join(valid) + "\n") if valid else "")
181	    return len(valid), det_by_fid
182	
183	
184	def _append_windows(fh, objs) -> None:
185	    """Append window records as JSONL and durably flush (bounded loss on crash)."""
186	    for o in objs:
187	        fh.write(json.dumps(o, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")
188	    fh.flush()
189	    os.fsync(fh.fileno())
190	
191	
192	def _atomic_write_trajectory(out_csv: str, rows) -> None:
193	    os.makedirs(osp.dirname(osp.abspath(out_csv)), exist_ok=True)
194	    tmp = out_csv + ".tmp"
195	    with open(tmp, "w", newline="") as f:
196	        w = csv.writer(f)
197	        w.writerow(["Frame", "Visibility", "X", "Y"])
198	        w.writerows(rows)
199	        f.flush()
200	        os.fsync(f.fileno())
201	    os.replace(tmp, out_csv)
202	
203	
204	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
205	# Inference
206	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
207	def run(frames_dir: str, weights: str, out_csv: str, sport: str = "badminton",
208	        limit: int = 0, batch_size: int = 8, num_workers: int = 4,
209	        log_every_batches: int = 50, cache_dir: "str | None" = None,
210	        flush_every: int = 1000, fresh: bool = False) -> str:
211	    import time
212	    import torch
213	    from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
214	    from detectors import build_detector
215	    from trackers import build_tracker
216	    from dataloaders import build_img_transforms, build_seq_transforms
217	    from dataloaders.dataset_loader import ImageDataset
218	    from utils import Center
219	
220	    cfg = _build_cfg(weights, sport)
221	    frames_in = int(cfg["model"]["frames_in"])
222	    input_wh = (int(cfg["model"]["inp_width"]), int(cfg["model"]["inp_height"]))
223	    output_wh = (int(cfg["model"]["out_width"]), int(cfg["model"]["out_height"]))
224	
225	    frames = sorted(glob.glob(osp.join(frames_dir, "*.png")) + glob.glob(osp.join(frames_dir, "*.jpg")))
226	    if limit:
227	        frames = frames[:limit]
228	    if len(frames) < frames_in:
229	        raise SystemExit(f"need >= {frames_in} frames, found {len(frames)} in {frames_dir}")
230	
231	    # Sliding windows of `frames_in` consecutive frames (the unit of GPU work + checkpoint).
232	    samples = []
233	    for i in range(len(frames) - frames_in + 1):
234	        window = frames[i:i + frames_in]
235	        annos = [{"frame_path": p, "center": Center(is_visible=False, x=-1.0, y=-1.0)} for p in window]
236	        samples.append({"frames": window, "annos": annos})
237	    windows_total = len(samples)
238	
239	    # ---- cache setup / resume decision ---------------------------------- #
240	    if cache_dir is None:
241	        cache_dir = default_cache_dir(out_csv)
242	    os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
243	    det_jsonl, _ = _cache_paths(cache_dir)
244	    manifest = None if fresh else read_manifest(cache_dir)
245	    resume = manifest_compatible(
246	        manifest or {}, frames_dir=frames_dir, weights=weights, sport=sport,
247	        frames_in=frames_in, frames_total=len(frames),
248	    )
249	    if resume:
250	        windows_done, det_by_fid = load_and_clean_cache(cache_dir)
251	        logger.info(f"resuming from cache: {windows_done}/{windows_total} windows already detected")
252	    else:
253	        if manifest is not None:
254	            logger.info("cache incompatible with this run -> starting fresh")
255	        reset_cache(cache_dir)
256	        windows_done, det_by_fid = 0, defaultdict(list)
257	
258	    base_manifest = {
259	        "version": CACHE_VERSION,
260	        "frames_dir": osp.abspath(frames_dir),
261	        "weights": weights,
262	        "sport": sport,
263	        "frames_in": frames_in,
264	        "frames_total": len(frames),
265	        "windows_total": windows_total,
266	        "windows_done": windows_done,
267	    }
268	    write_manifest(cache_dir, base_manifest)
269	
270	    # ---- detector pass over the un-cached windows ----------------------- #
271	    remaining = samples[windows_done:]
272	    if remaining:
273	        _, transform_test = build_img_transforms(cfg)
274	        try:
275	            _, seq_transform_test = build_seq_transforms(cfg)
276	        except Exception:
277	            seq_transform_test = None
278	        ds = ImageDataset(cfg, remaining, input_wh, output_wh,
279	                          transform=transform_test, seq_transform=seq_transform_test, is_train=False)
280	        loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False, num_workers=num_workers)
281	        detector = build_detector(cfg)
282	
283	        t0 = time.time()
284	        done = windows_done
285	        win_idx = windows_done
286	        log_every = max(1, log_every_batches)
287	        flush_n = max(1, flush_every)
288	        pending = []
289	        logger.info(f"detecting on {len(remaining)} remaining windows "
290	                    f"(total {windows_total}, batch={batch_size}, workers={num_workers})...")
291	        det_f = open(det_jsonl, "a")
292	        try:
293	            with torch.no_grad():
294	                for bi, batch in enumerate(loader):
295	                    imgs, _hms, trans = batch[0], batch[1], batch[2]
296	                    img_paths = [list(t) for t in batch[-1]]   # [frame_in][batch] -> path
297	                    batch_results, _ = detector.run_tensor(imgs, trans)
298	                    nb = imgs.shape[0]
299	                    for ib in range(nb):
300	                        frames_payload = []
301	                        for ie in sorted(batch_results[ib].keys()):
302	                            p = img_paths[ie][ib]
303	                            fid = _frame_id(p)
304	                            plain = [_det_plain(d) for d in batch_results[ib][ie]]
305	                            frames_payload.append([fid, plain])
306	                            det_by_fid[fid].extend(plain)
307	                        pending.append({"w": win_idx, "f": frames_payload})
308	                        win_idx += 1
309	                    done += nb
310	                    if len(pending) >= flush_n or done >= windows_total:
311	                        _append_windows(det_f, pending)
312	                        pending = []
313	                        base_manifest["windows_done"] = done
314	                        write_manifest(cache_dir, base_manifest)
315	                    if (bi + 1) % log_every == 0 or done >= windows_total:
316	                        el = time.time() - t0
317	                        new_done = done - windows_done
318	                        rate = new_done / el if el > 0 else 0.0
319	                        eta = (windows_total - done) / rate if rate > 0 else 0.0
320	                        logger.info(f"{done}/{windows_total} "
321	                                    f"({100 * done // max(1, windows_total)}%) | {rate:.1f} win/s | "
322	                                    f"elapsed {el:.0f}s | ETA {eta:.0f}s")
323	                if pending:
324	                    _append_windows(det_f, pending)
325	                    base_manifest["windows_done"] = done
326	                    write_manifest(cache_dir, base_manifest)
327	        finally:
328	            det_f.close()
329	        logger.info(f"detection done in {time.time() - t0:.0f}s; running tracker...")
330	    else:
331	        logger.info("detector cache already complete -> tracker-only re-run")
332	
333	    # ---- tracker re-run over the full ordered cache (cheap, deterministic) ---- #
334	    tracker = build_tracker(cfg)
335	    tracker.refresh()
336	    rows = []
337	    for fid in sorted(det_by_fid.keys()):
338	        r = tracker.update(_dets_to_tracker(det_by_fid[fid]))
339	        rows.append((fid, 1 if r.get("visi") else 0, r.get("x", -1), r.get("y", -1)))
340	
341	    _atomic_write_trajectory(out_csv, rows)
342	    visible = sum(1 for _, v, *_ in rows if v)
343	    logger.info(f"wrote {len(rows)} rows -> {out_csv}")
344	    logger.info(f"visible-shuttle frames: {visible}/{len(rows)}")
345	    return out_csv
346	
347	
348	def extract_frames(video_path: str, frames_dir: str) -> str:
349	    """Decode a video to PNG frames named by source frame index (00000000.png ...).
350	
351	    Idempotent: if the dir already holds the expected number of frames (matching the
352	    container's reported frame count) we skip re-decoding — re-extraction after a
353	    reboot is wasteful. NOTE: cv2 PNG extraction is slow; for big clips prefer
354	    extracting with ffmpeg on the host and passing --frames_dir.
355	    """
356	    import cv2
357	    os.makedirs(frames_dir, exist_ok=True)
358	    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
359	    if not cap.isOpened():
360	        raise SystemExit(f"cannot open video: {video_path}")
361	    expected = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT) or 0)
362	    existing = len(glob.glob(osp.join(frames_dir, "*.png")))
363	    if expected > 0 and existing >= expected:
364	        cap.release()
365	        logger.info(f"reusing {existing} already-extracted frames -> {frames_dir}")
366	        return frames_dir
367	    i = 0
368	    while True:
369	        ok, frame = cap.read()
370	        if not ok:
371	            break
372	        cv2.imwrite(osp.join(frames_dir, f"{i:08d}.png"), frame)
373	        i += 1
374	    cap.release()
375	    logger.info(f"extracted {i} frames -> {frames_dir}")
376	    return frames_dir
377	
378	
379	def main():
380	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
381	    ap.add_argument("--frames_dir", help="folder of pre-extracted frames")
382	    ap.add_argument("--video", help="video file; frames are extracted internally (in WSL)")
383	    ap.add_argument("--frames_out_dir", help="where to extract frames when --video is used")
384	    ap.add_argument("--weights", required=True)
385	    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
386	    ap.add_argument("--sport", default="badminton")
387	    ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=0, help="cap #frames (for quick tests)")
388	    ap.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=8)
389	    ap.add_argument("--num-workers", type=int, default=4)
390	    ap.add_argument("--log-every-batches", type=int, default=50, help="progress log cadence")
391	    ap.add_argument("--cache-dir", default=None,
392	                    help="resumable detector cache dir (default: <out>_wasbcache next to --out)")
393	    ap.add_argument("--flush-every", type=int, default=1000,
394	                    help="fsync the detector cache every N windows (bounds reboot loss)")
395	    ap.add_argument("--fresh", action="store_true", help="ignore any existing cache and start over")
396	    args = ap.parse_args()
397	
398	    frames_dir = args.frames_dir
399	    if args.video:
400	        frames_dir = extract_frames(args.video, args.frames_out_dir or (args.video + "_frames"))
401	    if not frames_dir:
402	        raise SystemExit("provide --frames_dir or --video")
403	    run(frames_dir, args.weights, args.out, args.sport, args.limit,
404	        batch_size=args.batch_size, num_workers=args.num_workers,
405	        log_every_batches=args.log_every_batches, cache_dir=args.cache_dir,
406	        flush_every=args.flush_every, fresh=args.fresh)
407	
408	
409	if __name__ == "__main__":
410	    main()
411

### 174. assistant (2026-06-13T08:25:53.465Z)

Now I have the real root cause. Let me lay out what the evidence shows:

- **No reboot** (last boot June 11) and **no sleep (S3) events** — but a chain of `Kernel-Power` **session transitions** ("transitioned from 51 to 52", reasons `InputHid`/`SessionUn`) around 22:53 last night. That's **Modern Standby (connected standby)**: when the machine sits idle with the screen off, Windows enters S0 low-power and the **Desktop Activity Moderator suspends Win32 processes and pauses the WSL2 VM**. That froze the Python orchestrator and the WSL inference mid-run — no clean exit, no timeout logged, frame cache frozen at exactly 30,087. `standby-timeout=0` doesn't prevent this; Modern Standby is screen-off-triggered.

The crucial good news from reading `wasb_infer.py`: the **GPU detector pass is checkpointed and resumable** (`det_raw.jsonl` + manifest, fsync'd every 1000 windows), and frame extraction is idempotent once complete. The killer was purely the machine idling — not a code bug.

Let me gather the exact numbers I need to choose the fastest extraction path, and install a keep-awake so this can't happen again.

### 179. assistant (2026-06-13T08:27:32.317Z)

# Holds a wake lock so Modern Standby (connected standby) can't suspend the
# long GPU run + WSL VM mid-flight. ES_CONTINUOUS persists only while THIS
# thread is alive, so we keep the process alive and re-assert periodically.
$sig = @'
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public class Caffeine {
  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
  public static extern uint SetThreadExecutionState(uint esFlags);
}
'@
Add-Type -TypeDefinition $sig
# ES_CONTINUOUS(0x80000000) | ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED(0x1) | ES_DISPLAY_REQUIRED(0x2)
$FLAGS = [uint32]"0x80000003"
$deadlineMin = 300  # ~5h ceiling; covers the whole pipeline with margin
$start = [DateTime]::UtcNow
while (([DateTime]::UtcNow - $start).TotalMinutes -lt $deadlineMin) {
    [void][Caffeine]::SetThreadExecutionState($FLAGS)
    Write-Output ("[keepawake] asserted ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED|ES_DISPLAY_REQUIRED at " + [DateTime]::Now.ToString("HH:mm:ss"))
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 60
}
# Release on clean exit.
[void][Caffeine]::SetThreadExecutionState([uint32]"0x80000000")
Write-Output "[keepawake] released"

### 196. assistant (2026-06-13T08:33:12.278Z)

Good — ffmpeg is extracting cleanly at ~52 fps, and I've confirmed the stitch line cuts from the **4K original** (`SOURCE_PATH`), not the proxy. Here's the full picture:

## What happened overnight

It wasn't a code bug — it was the **machine, twice**. The Kernel-Power event log shows **Modern Standby (connected standby)** transitions around 22:53 last night. When the machine sits idle with the screen off, Windows enters S0 low-power and **suspends Win32 processes and pauses the WSL2 VM**. That froze the Python orchestrator + WSL inference mid-run, with no clean exit (which is exactly why you saw no error — the log just stopped at "Running WASB inference" and the frame cache froze at 30,087). Note `standby-timeout=0` does *not* prevent this; Modern Standby is screen-off-triggered.

I also discovered *why it kept dying at the same place*: the run **never even reached the GPU** — it was still in **cv2 frame extraction** (the slow part — `wasb_infer.py`'s own docstring warns "cv2 PNG extraction is slow; for big clips prefer ffmpeg"). 46,080 frames at ~20 fps via cv2 = ~38 min of fragile single-threaded extraction, and standby kept killing it before inference began.

## Fixes applied

1. **Root cause — keep-awake.** A persistent wake-lock process (`SetThreadExecutionState(ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED|ES_DISPLAY_REQUIRED)`, re-asserted every 60s) is now holding the machine in S0 working state so standby can't suspend the run.
2. **The bottleneck — ffmpeg pre-extraction.** Extracting all 46,080 PNGs with ffmpeg natively inside WSL right now (~15 min, running). The frame count is provably exact (768.768s × 59.94fps = 46,080, CFR — matches cv2's count precisely), so the pipeline's slow cv2 step is **skipped entirely** and it goes straight to GPU inference (which is itself checkpointed/resumable).
3. **Stitch-from-original — confirmed.** The runner indexes the 1080p proxy but cuts the final reel from `F:\…\GX010125.MP4` (the 4K original) — same timeline, so timestamps transfer 1:1. I'll `ffprobe` the output to confirm it's 3840×2160 before calling it done.

Also: the **API `min_shot_count` fix you authorized is merged** ([#107](https://github.com/avidullu/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/107), squashed to master on green CI).

## What's next (automatic)

When extraction finishes (~15 min), I'll launch the pipeline — it'll skip extraction → **GPU WASB inference** → candidate windows → **Gemini rally confirmation** (serial, your API key's in `.env`) → **filter to shot_count > 7** → **stitch from 4K**. ETA after extraction: ~30–50 min. The live log stays at:

```powershell
Get-Content -Wait -Tail 40 C:\Users\avidu\Projects\badminton-highlight-indexer\output\gx010125_run.log
```

I'll report at each milestone and won't go idle on you.

### 245. assistant (2026-06-13T08:58:38.505Z)

"""Lightweight Gemini connectivity + quota preflight (no generation cost)."""
import os
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()
key = os.environ.get("GEMINI_API_KEY")
if not key:
    raise SystemExit("GEMINI_API_KEY not set")
print(f"key present: ...{key[-6:]} (len {len(key)})")

from google import genai

client = genai.Client(api_key=key)
models = list(client.models.list())
flash = [m.name for m in models if "flash-latest" in m.name]
print(f"models.list OK: {len(models)} models")
print(f"gemini-flash-latest available: {bool(flash)} -> {flash[:2]}")

196	        handoff_dir = os.path.join("output", f"chunks_{provider_name}_handoff")
